	news_event = { #尼亚加拉大劫案
		id = GEA_news.1
		immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event GEA_news.1" }
		title = GEA_news.1.t
		desc = GEA_news.1.d
		picture = GFX_GEA_news_1
		major = yes
		is_triggered_only = yes
		fire_only_once = yes
		option = {
			name = GEA_news.1.a #如此完美的手笔
			trigger = { tag = GEA }
		}
		option = {
			name = GEA_news.1.b #谁做的？
			trigger = { NOT = { tag = GEA } }
		}
	}

	country_event = {#1936 Situation
		id = GEA.0
		immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event GEA.0" }
		title = GEA.0.t
		desc = GEA.0.d
		picture = GFX_GEA_7

		fire_only_once = yes

		is_triggered_only = yes

		option = { #扬帆，启航！
			name = GEA.0.a
			hidden_effect = {
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = unit_ratio
					id = naval_bomber
					value = 25
				}
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = role_ratio
					id = marines
					value = 20
				}
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = equipment_production_factor
					id = armor
					value = -10
				}
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = equipment_production_min_factories
					id = fighter
					value = 1
				}
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = equipment_production_min_factories
					id = naval_bomber
					value = 1
				}
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = equipment_production_min_factories
					id = naval_bomber
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}

		option = { #那中国呢？

				id = GEA.1
			}
			hidden_effect = {

					value = 25
				}

				factor = 20

				}
			}

			}

	country_event = { #山东接管：本土三州由GEA完全控制后决定统治形式
		id = GEA.827
		title = GEA.827.t
		desc = GEA.827.d
		fire_only_once = yes
		trigger = {
			GEA = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_far_east_integrated } }
			tag = GEA
			controls_state = 597
			controls_state = 1064
			controls_state = 1065
		}
		immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event GEA.827" }

		option = {
			name = GEA.827.a
			ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = { GEA = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_far_east_integrated } } }
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					SQI = {
						transfer_state = 597 #济南
						transfer_state = 1064 #临沂
						transfer_state = 1065 #胶东
						set_capital = { state = 597 remember_old_capital = no }
						if = {
							limit = { is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GEA } }
							leave_faction = yes
						}
					}
					set_autonomy = {
						target = SQI
						autonomy_state = kr_default_puppet
						end_wars = yes
						end_civil_wars = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { is_in_faction = yes SQI = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GEA } } }
						random_country = {
							limit = { is_faction_leader = yes is_in_faction_with = GEA }
							add_to_faction = SQI
						}
					}
					SQI = {
						clr_country_flag = GEA_china_warlord_interaction_blocked
						clr_country_flag = GEA_german_coup_defeated
						remove_all_ministers = yes
						add_ideas = {
							SQI_Zhu_Qinglan_hog_ade
							SQI_Ding_Shujing_for_ade
							SQI_Cao_Fulin_eco_ade
							SQI_Qu_Yingguang_sec_ade
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					GEA = {
						if = {
							limit = { NOT = { SQI = { has_character = GEA_Tang_Yangdu } } }
							GEA_Tang_Yangdu = { set_nationality = SQI }
						}
					}
				}
				SQI = {
					add_popularity = { ideology = authoritarian_democrat popularity = 0.30 }
					set_politics = { ruling_party = authoritarian_democrat elections_allowed = no }
					promote_character = { character = GEA_Tang_Yangdu ideology = authdem_kleptocracy_subtype }
					set_party_name = {
						ideology = authoritarian_democrat
						name = GEA_SQI_maintenance_party
						long_name = GEA_SQI_maintenance_party_long
					}
					set_cosmetic_tag = GEA_SQI_maintenance
					hidden_effect = {
						GEA_Tang_Yangdu = { unit_leader_event = { id = GEA.840 days = 2 } }
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					GEA = {
						set_variable = { GEA_china_sqi_attitude = 100 }
						set_variable = { GEA_china_sqi_capital_influence = 90 }
						set_variable = { GEA_china_sqi_military_projection = 90 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		option = {
			name = GEA.827.b
			ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = { GEA = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_far_east_integrated } } }
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					SQI = {
						transfer_state = 597 #济南

						transfer_state = 1065 #胶东

							limit = { is_faction_leader = yes is_in_faction_with = GEA }
							add_to_faction = SQI
						}
					}
					SQI = {

						clr_country_flag = GEA_german_coup_defeated
						remove_all_ministers = yes

						}
					}

	country_event = { #王克敏与华北临时政府
		id = GEA.828
		immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event GEA.828" }
		title = GEA.828.t
		desc = GEA.828.d
		fire_only_once = yes

		trigger = {
			GEA = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_far_east_integrated } }
			tag = GEA
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = GEA_Submission }
			OR = {
				QIE = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject_of = GEA

			OR = {
				XSM = { exists = yes is_subject_of = GEA }
				AND = {
					controls_state = 283
					controls_state = 348
					controls_state = 353
					controls_state = 604
					controls_state = 616
					controls_state = 621
					controls_state = 774
				}
			}
		}
		immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event GEA.847" }

		option = {
			name = GEA.847.a
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = { GEA = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GEA_far_east_integrated } } }
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					every_state = {
						limit = {
							OR = { state = 283 state = 348 state = 353 state = 604 state = 616 state = 621 state = 774 }
							is_controlled_by = GEA
						}
						XSM = { transfer_state = PREV }
					}
					XSM = {
						if = {
							limit = { owns_state = 348 }
							set_capital = { state = 348 remember_old_capital = no }
						}
						if = {
							limit = { is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GEA } }
							leave_faction = yes
						}
					}
					set_autonomy = {
						target = XSM
						autonomy_state = kr_default_puppet
						end_wars = yes
						end_civil_wars = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { is_in_faction = yes XSM = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GEA } } }
						faction_leader = { add_to_faction = XSM }
					}
					XSM = {
						clr_country_flag = GEA_china_warlord_interaction_blocked
						clr_country_flag = GEA_german_coup_defeated
						set_country_flag = GEA_xsm_puppet_administration_established
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					GEA = {
						if = {
							limit = { NOT = { XSM = { has_character = GEA_Ma_Hongkui } } }
							GEA_Ma_Hongkui = { set_nationality = XSM }
						}
					}
				}
				XSM = {
					add_popularity = { ideology = paternal_autocrat popularity = 0.30 }
					set_politics = { ruling_party = paternal_autocrat elections_allowed = no }
					promote_character = { character = GEA_Ma_Hongkui ideology = pataut_warlordism_subtype }
					set_party_name = {
						ideology = paternal_autocrat
						name = GEA_XSM_german_party
						long_name = GEA_XSM_german_party_long
					}
					set_cosmetic_tag = GEA_XSM_german
					hidden_effect = {
						remove_ideas_with_trait = head_of_government
						add_ideas = XSM_Ma_Hongkui_hog_pau
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	country_event = { #调停暹缅战争：向进攻方提出白和平议
		id = GEA.1113
		immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event GEA.1113" }
		title = GEA.1113.t
		desc = GEA.1113.d

		is_triggered_only = yes
		trigger = {
			country_exists = GEA
			OR = {
				AND = {
					tag = SIA
					has_offensive_war_with = BRM
				}
				AND = {
					tag = BRM
					has_offensive_war_with = SIA
				}
			}
		}

		option = {
			name = GEA.1113.a
			ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					tag = SIA
					has_war_with = BRM
				}
				BRM = { save_event_target_as = white_peace_receiver }
				white_peace_with_TARGET = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						BRM = { 
							exists = yes 
							NOT = { 
								is_in_faction_with = GER 
							} 
						}
						GER = { 
							exists = yes 
							is_in_faction = yes 
						}
					}
					BRM = {
						if = {
							limit = { 
								is_in_faction = yes
							}
							leave_faction = yes
						}
					}
					GER = { add_to_faction = BRM }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					tag = BRM
					has_war_with = SIA
				}
				SIA = { save_event_target_as = white_peace_receiver }
				white_peace_with_TARGET = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						BRM = { exists = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER } }
						GER = { exists = yes is_in_faction = yes }
					}
					BRM = {
						if = {
							limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
							leave_faction = yes
						}
					}
					GER = { add_to_faction = BRM }
				}
			}
		}
		option = {
			name = GEA.1113.b
			ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SIA has_war_with = BRM }
				GEA = { country_event = { id = GEA.1114 days = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
